Source answer: 90 degrees

Candidate hash: f3b17d66006ac8cf54fc2d4944315b596ab36964a380bc0fe6dfb05202453dd6

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Electric Filing - Shortening the Nails (printed p. 242; PDF p. 266)

At what angle should an electric file be held to cut or shorten acrylic?

用电动磨甲机切割或缩短丙烯酸甲时，应保持什么角度？

  1. a. 90 degrees
    90度 ✓ Correct (authored)
  2. b. 30 degrees
    30度 (authored)
  3. c. 60 degrees
    60度 (authored)
  4. d. 45 degrees
    45度 (authored)

Why / 为什么
Hold the electric file straight against the end of the acrylic nail. It cuts across the end instead of shaping the top.
把电动磨甲机垂直对准丙烯酸甲的前端。这样会横向磨短前端，而不是打磨甲面形状。

Lock this / 记重点
90 degrees = hold the file straight to the nail end.
90 度 = 磨甲机垂直对准指甲前端。

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Manicuring - Massage Movements (printed p. 162; PDF p. 186)

What is the quick tapping movement called?

快速轻拍的动作称为什么？

  1. a. Effleurage
    轻抚法 (authored)
  2. b. Tapotement
    叩击法 ✓ Correct (authored)
  3. c. Petrissage
    揉捏法 (authored)
  4. d. Vibration
    振动 (authored)

Why / 为什么
Tapotement uses quick, light tapping or striking movements. It stimulates the area instead of using the slow gliding strokes of effleurage.
tapotement（叩击法）使用快速、轻柔的拍打动作，作用偏刺激，不同于 effleurage 的缓慢滑动。

Lock this / 记重点
tapotement = quick tapping.
tapotement = 快速叩击。

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Electric Filing - Shaping an Enhancement (printed p. 242-248; PDF p. 266)

At what angle should an electric file be held to file and shape acrylic?

用电动磨甲机打磨和修整丙烯酸甲时，应保持什么角度？

  1. a. 90 degrees
    90度 (authored)
  2. b. 60 degrees
    60度 (authored)
  3. c. 30 degrees
    30度 ✓ Correct (authored)
  4. d. 45 degrees
    45度 (authored)

Why / 为什么
Hold the electric file at about 30 degrees while shaping the enhancement. This lets the bit meet the surface without cutting straight into it.
塑形人工甲时，把电动磨甲机保持约 30 度。这样磨头能接触表面，而不会垂直切入产品。

Lock this / 记重点
30 degrees = shape the enhancement surface.
30 度 = 塑形人工甲表面。

Source answer: Primer

Source audit note: The source keys primer as the substance that causes heat during acrylic application. Heat is produced by the exothermic polymerization reaction, not by primer, so the keyed answer is not safe to teach.

Candidate hash: e3e88585de342fe005911c5cef4feb1f275b887f64789d9392e390b848836801

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Electric Filing - Shortening the Nails (printed p. 242; PDF p. 266)

At what angle should an electric file be held to shorten an enhancement?

用电动磨甲机缩短美甲增强层时，应保持什么角度？

  1. a. 60 degrees
    60度 (authored)
  2. b. 45 degrees
    45度 (authored)
  3. c. 30 degrees
    30度 (authored)
  4. d. 90 degrees
    90度 ✓ Correct (authored)

Why / 为什么
For shortening, the bit works across the nail's end instead of over the top surface. A 90-degree position keeps that cut straight.
修短时，磨头横向作用于指甲前端，而不是甲面。保持 90 度能让前端切口平直。

Lock this / 记重点
90 degrees = shorten the nail end.
90 度 = 修短指甲前端。

Which nail enhancement covers the whole nail plate and can help protect bitten nails?

哪种美甲增强方式可覆盖整个甲板，并帮助保护被咬过的指甲？

  1. a. A small silk patch on one crack
    只在一处裂缝上贴小块丝绸贴片 (authored)
  2. b. A partial-well nail tip
    部分贴合区甲片 (authored)
  3. c. Full nail tips
    全贴甲片 ✓ Correct (authored)
  4. d. A nail art decal
    美甲贴花 (authored)

Why / 为什么
A full-cover tip extends over the whole nail plate. It can create a smooth protective covering when a bitten nail has very little free edge.
全覆盖甲片会盖住整个甲板。被咬指甲几乎没有游离缘时，它能形成平整的保护性覆盖。

Lock this / 记重点
full-cover tip = covers the whole nail plate.
全覆盖甲片 = 覆盖整个甲板。

Candidate hash: 6829cb655b1a413628780ea3a3d0129ccbe7a0ed9cdb65ec42ee3b50c222c2a5

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Manicuring - Soaking the Nails (printed p. 172-178; PDF p. 196)

What is the purpose of soaking nails in water?

将指甲浸泡在水中的目的是什么？

  1. a. To remove an acrylic enhancement
    卸除丙烯酸人工甲 (authored)
  2. b. To harden cured gel
    使已固化的凝胶变硬 (authored)
  3. c. To soften the area around the cuticle
    软化甲小皮周围的区域 ✓ Correct (authored)
  4. d. To disinfect metal tools
    消毒金属工具 (authored)

Why / 为什么
Warm water softens dead tissue around the nail plate. This makes gentle cuticle care easier without forcing or scraping living skin.
温水能软化甲板周围的死皮，使甲小皮护理更容易，也不必用力推刮活皮肤。

Lock this / 记重点
nail soak = soften tissue before gentle care.
泡甲 = 在轻柔护理前软化组织。

Authority

  • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Nail Structure - Nail Plate (printed p. 99-100; PDF p. 123)

What part of the nail lies on the nail bed?

指甲的哪一部分位于甲床上？

  1. a. Nail bed
    甲床 (authored)
  2. b. Nail mantle
    甲套 (authored)
  3. c. Nail body
    甲体 ✓ Correct (authored)
  4. d. Nail matrix
    甲母质 (authored)

Why / 为什么
The nail body is the visible nail plate. It rests on the nail bed and moves forward as new nail cells grow.
nail body（甲体）就是看得见的甲板。它位于甲床上，并随着新甲细胞生长而向前移动。

Lock this / 记重点
nail body = visible plate on the nail bed.
nail body = 位于甲床上的可见甲板。

What does the MSDS (Material Safety Data Sheet) mention?

安全数据表（MSDS）提到什么？

  2. b. Employee schedules

Candidate hash: bd9e4a6bcb12c62b4c4c681f0ce080cd13f07d2766d9173b79abb609f8610b75

Authority

  • Milady Standard Foundations - Infection Control - Step 2: Disinfecting (printed p. 118; PDF p. 613)

What is the act of cleaning a dry, non-absorbent surface called?

清洁干燥、非吸收性表面的操作称为什么？

  1. a. Infection
    感染 (authored)
  2. b. Sanitation
    清洁/卫生处理 (authored)
  3. c. Immunity
    免疫力 (authored)
  4. d. Disinfection
    消毒 ✓ Correct (authored)

Why / 为什么
Disinfection means using a chemical to kill most germs on a hard surface after the surface has been cleaned.
disinfection（消毒）是指先把坚硬表面清洁干净，再用化学消毒剂杀死上面的大多数细菌。

Lock this / 记重点
clean the hard surface first; disinfect it second.
先清洁坚硬表面，再进行消毒。

    • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Nail Product Chemistry - Coatings That Harden by Evaporation (printed p. 121-122; PDF p. 145)

    When nail polish is left in an open bottle, causing it to evaporate and dry, what is this phenomenon?

    指甲油瓶打开后挥发并变干，这是什么现象？

    1. a. A physical phenomenon
      物理现象 ✓ Correct (authored)
    2. b. A chemical reaction
      化学反应 (authored)
    3. c. A biological infection
      生物感染 (authored)
    4. d. A sterilization process

    Why / 为什么

    甲油中的液体变成蒸气离开后，会留下固体薄膜。材料的形态改变了，但没有产生新物质。

    Lock this / 记重点
    evaporation changes form, not chemical identity.
    蒸发改变形态，不改变物质本身。

    A client with acrylic nails complains that the product has peeled off from the skin. What is likely the issue?

    1. a. The product was kept away from the skin

      使用了正确的粉液比例 (authored)

    Why / 为什么
    Product touching skin cannot bond correctly to the nail plate. As the skin moves and sheds, it pulls the attached product up and causes lifting.
    碰到皮肤的产品不能正确黏附甲板。皮肤活动和脱落时，会把相连的产品拉起，造成翘起。

    Lock this / 记重点
    product on skin = lifting risk.
    产品碰到皮肤 = 容易翘起。

    1. a. A liquid

    epidermis = 皮肤外层。

    To ensure better adhesion of the product to the nail, what should be done?

    为了让产品更好地附着在指甲上，应做什么？

    1. a. Keep the nails clean and dry
      保持指甲清洁干燥 ✓ Correct (authored)
    2. b. Leave oil on the nail plate
      让油留在甲板上 (authored)
    3. c. Begin while the nail is still damp
      指甲仍潮湿时开始操作 (authored)
    4. d. Skip removing dust from the nail

    Why / 为什么

    油、水和粉尘会挡住产品接触甲板。清洁、干燥的表面能让产品直接接触并黏得更牢。

    Lock this / 记重点
    a better bond starts with a clean, dry nail.
    更牢黏附从清洁、干燥的指甲开始。

    To avoid creating a "fire ring" with an electric file, how should it be used?

    3. c. Petrissage
      揉捏法 (authored)
    4. d. Vibration
      振动 (authored)

    Why / 为什么

    tapotement（叩击法）是一连串快速拍打动作，比缓慢滑动的按摩动作更有刺激性。

    Lock this / 记重点
    rapid tapping = tapotement.
    快速叩击 = tapotement。

    • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Chemistry and Chemical Safety - The pH Scale (printed p. 165-167; PDF p. 660)

    A pH scale of 7 indicates a neutral solution such as pure water. What does the pH scale tell you?

    pH值7表示像纯水这样的中性溶液。pH值表示什么？

    1. a. Evaporation rate
      蒸发速度 (authored)
    2. b. Acidity or Alkalinity
      酸性或碱性 ✓ Correct (authored)
    3. c. Viscosity
      黏度 (authored)
    4. d. Solubility
      溶解度 (authored)

    Why / 为什么
    The pH scale shows whether a water-based product is acidic or alkaline. Seven is neutral; lower numbers are acidic and higher numbers are alkaline.
    pH 值表示含水产品偏酸还是偏碱。7 是中性，低于 7 为酸性，高于 7 为碱性。

    Lock this / 记重点
    pH below 7 = acid; above 7 = alkaline.
    pH 低于 7 = 酸性；高于 7 = 碱性。

      电动磨甲机应该如何握持：

      • Milady Standard Foundations - Infection Control - Removing Disposable Gloves (printed p. 127; PDF p. 622)

      The correct way to remove blood-contaminated gloves is to:

      正确脱下被血液污染的手套的方法是：

      1. a. Touch the outside surface directly
        直接触摸外表面 (source)
      2. b. Remove both gloves at the same time
        同时脱下两只手套 (source)
      3. c. Remove one at a time, turning them inside out
        一次脱一只，并将其翻到里面 ✓ Correct (source)
      4. d. Wipe and reuse them
        擦拭后重复使用 (source)

      逐只把手套由内向外翻，可以避免脏的外侧碰到裸露皮肤，也能把两只用过的手套包在一起丢弃。

      Lock this / 记重点
      remove gloves inside out; touch only the clean side.
      脱手套时由内向外翻，只碰干净的一面。

      Which of the following MOST accurately explains the relationship between direct and indirect transmission of disease?

      以下哪一项最准确地解释了疾病直接传播与间接传播之间的关系？

      1. a. Neither direct nor indirect transmission require contact with another person.
        直接传播和间接传播都不需要与他人接触。 (source)
      2. b. Direct transmission requires contact with another person whereas indirect transmission does not.
        直接传播需要与他人接触，而间接传播不需要。 ✓ Correct (source)
      3. c. Direct transmission does not require contact with another person whereas indirect transmission does.
        直接传播不需要与他人接触，而间接传播需要。 (source)

      direct transmission（直接传播）通过人与人接触发生；indirect transmission（间接传播）通过受污染物品、表面、食物或其他中间来源发生。

      Lock this / 记重点
      direct = person contact; indirect = contaminated middle source.
      直接 = 人与人接触；间接 = 受污染的中间来源。

      practice-comprehensive-018 · approved · source
      Source and review evidence

      Source stem: What is the difference between respiratory droplets and airborne transmission?

      Source answer: Respiratory droplets do not stay in the air as long.

      Source audit note: None

      Answer review
      pass
      Teaching review
      pass
      English review
      pass
      Chinese review
      pass
      Dispute
      none

      Candidate hash: e611698c1013eacf7efdebb346ea4eeb7e79e4b1813b9c5a623462a97d30b0ec

      Authority

      • Milady Standard Foundations - Infection Control - Droplet and Airborne Transmission (printed p. 100-103; PDF p. 595)

      What is the difference between respiratory droplets and airborne transmission?

      呼吸道飞沫传播与空气传播有什么区别？

      1. a. Respiratory droplets do not stay in the air as long.
        呼吸道飞沫不会在空气中停留很长时间。 ✓ Correct (source)
      2. b. Airborne transmission involves wetter particles which are therefore less likely to be transmitted.
        空气传播涉及较湿的颗粒，因此传播可能性较小。 (source)
      3. c. Only airborne transmission is guaranteed to make individuals ill.
        只有空气传播才会使人患病。 (source)
      4. d. Respiratory droplets are smaller particles and therefore more likely to be transmitted.
        呼吸道飞沫是较小的颗粒，因此更容易传播。 (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Large respiratory droplets fall from the air fairly quickly. Smaller airborne material can remain floating longer and travel farther.
      较大的呼吸飞沫会较快落下；更小的空气传播物可悬浮更久，也能传播更远。

      Lock this / 记重点
      droplets fall sooner; airborne material stays longer.
      飞沫较快落下；空气传播物停留更久。

      沙龙消毒剂需要有杀菌范围和安全用法的标签说明，但不要求保证完全不会引起过敏。

      Lock this / 记重点
      disinfectant requirement = germ-killing claims, not allergy-free.
      消毒剂要求 = 杀菌说明，不是保证无过敏。

      1. a. By the later part of the twentieth century, nail technicians were taking color inspiration from movie stars, whereas in the earlier part of the century, they were not using color at all in nail care.
        到 20 世纪后期，美甲师开始从电影明星身上汲取色彩灵感，而世纪前期的指甲护理完全不使用色彩。 (source)

        20 世纪前期，人们不做每周手部护理；但到了 20 世纪后半期，这项服务的受欢迎程度急剧上升。 (source)

        丙烯酸甲片、精细指甲彩绘和 UV 凝胶等人工甲增强在 20 世纪后期流行起来，而世纪前期的技术与手法较为有限。 ✓ Correct (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Early nail services focused mainly on natural-nail care and simple polish. Later products added tips, acrylic, gel, and detailed nail art.
      早期美甲主要护理天然指甲并使用简单甲油；后来出现甲片、丙烯酸、凝胶和精细美甲艺术。

      Lock this / 记重点
      later nail history = more enhancement and art choices.
      后期美甲历史 = 更多人工甲和艺术选择。

      Authority

      • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Nail Product Chemistry - Dangers of Over-Filing (printed p. 119-120; PDF p. 143)

      Overfiling the nail will make it:

      过度锉磨会使指甲：

      1. a. excessively thick and smooth
        过厚且光滑 (source)
      2. b. excessively thin and impermeable
        过薄且不透水 (source)
      3. c. excessively thick and porous
        过厚且多孔 (source)
      4. d. excessively thin and porous
        过薄且多孔 ✓ Correct (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Overfiling removes too many nail-plate layers. Porous means having more tiny openings, so the thinned nail absorbs liquids more easily and becomes weak.
      过度修锉会去掉太多甲板层。porous 表示有更多微小孔隙，因此变薄的指甲更容易吸收液体，也更脆弱。

      Lock this / 记重点
      overfiling = thin, weak nail with more tiny openings.
      过度修锉 = 指甲变薄、变弱、孔隙增多。

      Which is the best reasoning for why a dry manicure must be performed prior to nail tip application?

      为什么粘贴甲片前必须进行干式修甲？以下哪项理由最恰当？

      1. a. It is not necessary for a dry manicure to be performed before a nail tip application; it is a special luxury service that can be added on.
        粘贴甲片前不必进行干式修甲；这只是一项可加选的特别奢华服务。 (source)
      2. b. If any leftover nail polish is left on the nail, it will show through on the new extension.
        如果甲面留有任何指甲油，都会透过新的延长甲显现出来。 (source)

      甲面残留的油、水、粉尘或死皮会让甲片黏不牢；这些残留物一旦翘起，甲片也会翘起。

      Lock this / 记重点

      指甲清洁、干燥 = 甲片黏得更牢。

      • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Nail Tips - Removal (printed p. 264-267; PDF p. 288)

      After removing the nail tip from the natural nail, it is recommended that you ______to remove any adhesive residue.

      从天然甲上卸除甲片后，建议_____以清除残留的粘合剂。

      1. a. have the client wash their hands with soap and warm water
        让顾客用肥皂和温水洗手 (source)
      2. b. gently buff the nail with a medium buffer
        用中目抛光锉轻轻抛磨指甲 (source)
      3. c. immerse the nail in acetone polish remover
        将指甲浸入含丙酮的卸甲水中 (source)

        用细目抛光锉轻轻抛磨指甲 ✓ Correct (source)

      Why / 为什么
      A fine buffer smooths small adhesive remains without taking off too much natural nail. Heavy filing can thin and damage the plate.
      细砂抛光锉能磨平少量残胶，又不会去掉太多天然甲板。用力粗锉会使甲板变薄受损。

      Lock this / 记重点
      tip removal residue = gentle fine buffer.
      卸甲片后的残胶 = 轻柔使用细砂抛光锉。

      Authority

      • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Nail Wraps and Resins - Viscosity and Cure (printed p. 286-305; PDF p. 310)

      Which describes the relationship between viscosity and curing time when it comes to resins?

      就树脂而言，黏度与固化时间之间有什么关系？

      1. a. Thick or thin viscosity resins are quick to cure.
        无论黏度高低，树脂都能快速固化。 (source)
      2. b. Cure time for all resins-thick or thin-tends to be about 48 hours.
        所有树脂无论黏度高低，固化时间通常都约为48小时。 (source)
      3. c. The thinner the viscosity, the faster cure time; the thicker the viscosity, the longer the cure time.
        黏度越低，固化越快；黏度越高，固化越慢。 ✓ Correct (source)
      4. d. The thicker the viscosity, the faster the cure time; the thinner the viscosity, the slower the cure time.
        黏度越高，固化越快；黏度越低，固化越慢。 (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Thin resin spreads easily and exposes more material to moisture, so it hardens faster. Thick resin moves slowly and generally needs more curing time.
      稀树脂容易铺开，更多材料接触水分，因此硬化较快。稠树脂流动慢，通常需要更长固化时间。

      Lock this / 记重点
      thin resin = faster cure; thick resin = slower cure.
      稀树脂 = 固化较快；稠树脂 = 固化较慢。

      • Milady Standard Foundations - Infection Control - Sterilization (printed p. 118; PDF p. 613)

      Which one of the following is TRUE?

      1. a. Sterilization destroys all microbial life except for spores
        灭菌会消灭除芽孢以外的所有微生物生命。 (source)
      2. b. Sterilization is usually mandated in the beauty and wellness industry
        美容与健康行业通常获准采用消毒。 (source)

      Lock this / 记重点

      灭菌会杀死所有细菌，包括芽孢。

      • Milady Standard Foundations - Infection Control - Disinfectable Surfaces (printed p. 118-123; PDF p. 613)

      Which of the following is NOT one of the cautionary tips to consider when handling disinfectants?

      以下哪一项不是使用消毒剂时应牢记的安全提示？

      1. a. Avoid skin and eye contact
        避免接触皮肤和眼睛。 (source)
      2. b. Use tongs to remove implements from disinfectants
        使用夹钳从消毒剂中取出器具。 (source)
      3. c. Wear gloves and safety glasses
        佩戴手套和护目镜。 (source)

      Lock this / 记重点
      disinfectant: cleaned hard surface only.
      消毒剂：只用于已清洁的坚硬表面。

      When salt is dissolved into water, _______.

      1. a. salt and water are both solvents
        盐和水都是溶剂 (source)
      2. b. salt is the solute and water is the solvent
        盐是溶质，水是溶剂 ✓ Correct (source)
      3. c. water is the solute and salt is the solvent

      4. d. salt and water are both solutes

      • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Chemistry and Chemical Safety - Miscible and Immiscible Liquids (printed p. 159-160; PDF p. 654)

        既相溶又互不相溶 (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Miscible liquids mix evenly and stay mixed. Immiscible liquids separate into layers, as oil and water do.

      • Milady Standard Nail Technology 8th Edition - Skin Inflammations - Cellulitis (printed p. 87-89; PDF p. 111)

      Which is NOT true regarding skin inflammations?

      关于皮肤炎症，以下哪项不正确？

      1. a. Psoriasis is caused by skin cells that turn over faster than normal.
        银屑病由皮肤细胞更新速度快于正常速度引起。 (source)
      2. b. Cellulitis is highly contagious and can spread from one person to another very easily.
        蜂窝织炎传染性很强，很容易在人与人之间传播。 ✓ Correct (source)
      3. c. Eczema is an inflammatory disease than can cause painful itching and is either acute or chronic
        湿疹是一种炎症性疾病，可引起疼痛性瘙痒，并有急性或慢性之分。 (source)

        银屑病可影响甲板，也可影响皮肤。 (source)

      Why / 为什么
      Cellulitis is a bacterial infection in deeper skin tissue. It needs medical care, but ordinary person-to-person contact does not spread it easily.
      cellulitis（蜂窝织炎）是较深皮肤组织的细菌感染，需要医疗处理，但一般接触不容易在人与人之间传播。

      Lock this / 记重点
      cellulitis = serious infection, not highly contagious.
      cellulitis = 严重感染，但不是高度传染。
